+/// The API may redact the called gene to `Unknown` when it abstains. The
+/// audited capability registry still identifies which gene's evidence matters.
+String geneForResult(PerDrugResult result) {
+  final String reported = result.pharmacogenomicProfile.primaryGene;
+  if (reported.isNotEmpty && reported.toLowerCase() != 'unknown') {
+    return reported;
+  }
+  return capabilityForDrug(result.drug)?.primaryGene ?? reported;
+}
+
+/// An explicit backend abstention is a reason, even when it fits none of the
+/// four more specific UI categories. Do not claim no reason was recorded.
+String unknownExplanation(PerDrugResult result, UnknownReason reason) {
+  if (reason == UnknownReason.unspecified &&
+      result.clinicalRecommendation.action.trim().isNotEmpty) {
+    return result.clinicalRecommendation.action;
+  }
+  return reason.explanation;

+## Resource ownership and privacy boundary
+
+Both upload endpoints reject a declared oversized file before reading it and
+otherwise read in 64 KiB chunks only through the 5 MiB cap. Decompression has a
+separate 100 MiB output cap. Validation and coverage parsing run off the event
+loop. The 5 MiB input limit comes from the measured PGx input shape: a complete
+seven-gene file is roughly 200 KiB, while whole-genome VCFs must be subset before
+upload. The decompression allowance preserves the existing 20× safety margin;
+it is not an assay-quality threshold.
+
+Each PharmCAT invocation owns a process group. Normal completion, timeout, and
+request cancellation all wait/reap that group; cancellation cleanup and removal
+of the per-request temporary tree finish before the JVM concurrency permit is
+returned. PharmCAT/logback runs with that directory as its working directory, so
+tool logs are removed with the synthetic/genomic working files rather than being
+written beside the application.
+
+The separate explanation guard log defaults to
+`backend/logs/guard_violations.jsonl` (or the explicit `GUARD_LOG_PATH`). It may
+contain drug, gene, phenotype, result label, and rejected explanation prose for
+review; it does not receive the uploaded VCF, sample identifier, diplotype, or
+detected-variant list. Reverse-proxy request logs are outside this application
+logger and must be configured by the host.
+
+The application does not persist uploaded VCFs or results. Static/default mode
+makes no outbound explanation request. This guarantee covers application-owned
+memory and temporary files; it does not claim control over reverse-proxy access
+logs, platform crash dumps, backups, or host-level retention. `live` remains
+disabled for runtime clinical prose by the approval policy and must not be used
+to make a blanket statement about data locality if its implementation changes.
+
+Forwarded client identity is ignored by default. Configure
+`TRUSTED_PROXY_CIDRS` only with direct proxy networks whose forwarding headers
+are sanitized, and configure the ASGI server's forwarded-header trust to the
+same boundary. Otherwise rate limiting intentionally groups traffic by the
+direct peer rather than trusting a caller-selected identity.
+

@@ -58,6 +65,67 @@ _NO_CALL = frozenset({".", "./.", ".|.", ""})
 _HOM_REF = frozenset({"0/0", "0|0", "0"})
 
 
+class EvidenceStatus(str, Enum):
+    """Application evidence contract for one required genomic position."""
+
+    VALID = "valid_observed_genotype"
+    PARTIAL = "partial_genotype"
+    NO_CALL = "no_call"
+    GT_NOT_PROVIDED = "gt_not_provided"
+    FILTER_FAILED = "filter_failed"
+    QUALITY_NOT_ESTABLISHED = "quality_not_established"
+    MALFORMED = "malformed_genotype"
+    ABSENT = "absent_position"
+
+
+# If duplicate rows disagree, the less trustworthy state wins. A second PASS
+# row must never erase an explicit failure at the same coordinate.
+_STATUS_PRIORITY = {
+    EvidenceStatus.VALID: 0,
+    EvidenceStatus.NO_CALL: 1,
+    EvidenceStatus.PARTIAL: 2,
+    EvidenceStatus.GT_NOT_PROVIDED: 3,
+    EvidenceStatus.QUALITY_NOT_ESTABLISHED: 4,
+    EvidenceStatus.MALFORMED: 5,
+    EvidenceStatus.FILTER_FAILED: 6,
+}
+
+
+def _sample_evidence(parts: list[str]) -> tuple[EvidenceStatus, str]:
+    """Classify the first (and, after validation, only) sample at a VCF row.
+
+    This deliberately does not invent DP, GQ or QUAL thresholds. The contract
+    trusts an upstream explicit ``FILTER=PASS``, declines named filter failures,
+    and preserves ``FILTER=.`` as a distinct quality-not-established state that
+    remains usable with a visible warning. GT is located through FORMAT as
+    required by the VCF specification.
+    """
+    format_keys = parts[8].split(":")
+    try:
+        gt_index = format_keys.index("GT")
+    except ValueError:
+        return EvidenceStatus.GT_NOT_PROVIDED, ""
+
+    sample_values = parts[9].split(":")
+    genotype = sample_values[gt_index].strip() if gt_index < len(sample_values) else ""
+
+    if genotype in _NO_CALL:
+        return EvidenceStatus.NO_CALL, genotype
+
+    alleles = re.split(r"[/|]", genotype)
+    if "." in alleles:
+        return EvidenceStatus.PARTIAL, genotype
+    if not alleles or any(not allele.isdigit() for allele in alleles):
+        return EvidenceStatus.MALFORMED, genotype
+
+    filter_value = parts[6].strip()
+    if filter_value.upper() == "PASS":
+        return EvidenceStatus.VALID, genotype
+    if filter_value in {"", "."}:
+        return EvidenceStatus.QUALITY_NOT_ESTABLISHED, genotype
+    return EvidenceStatus.FILTER_FAILED, genotype
+
+

     """Required positions and measured thresholds. Cached; cache_clear in tests."""

-def readiness(report: CoverageReport, drug_gene: dict[str, str]) -> dict:
+@dataclass(frozen=True)
+class DrugEligibility:
+    """Whether all declared input dependencies for one drug are usable."""
+
+    drug: str
+    required_genes: tuple[str, ...]
+    failed_genes: tuple[str, ...]
+    guidance_capable: bool
+    limitation: str
+
+    @property
+    def eligible(self) -> bool:
+        return (
+            bool(self.required_genes)
+            and self.guidance_capable
+            and not self.failed_genes
+        )
+
+    @property
+    def valid_genes(self) -> tuple[str, ...]:
+        failed = set(self.failed_genes)
+        return tuple(gene for gene in self.required_genes if gene not in failed)
+
+    @property
+    def reason(self) -> str:
+        if self.eligible:
+            return ""
+        if self.limitation and not self.guidance_capable:
+            return self.limitation
+        if not self.required_genes:
+            return (
+                "This drug is outside the audited application scope; no "
+                "application guidance is produced."
+            )
+        return (
+            "Required genomic evidence is incomplete for "
+            + ", ".join(self.failed_genes)
+            + ". Available partner findings are partial evidence only; no complete "
+              "drug-level PGx recommendation is made."
+        )
+
+
+def drug_eligibility(
+    report: CoverageReport,
+    drug: str,
+    mapping: dict | None = None,
+) -> DrugEligibility:
+    """Shared drug-level evidence decision used by preview and final analysis."""
+    capability = capability_for(drug)
+    required = capability.evidence_dependencies if capability else ()
+    failed = tuple(
+        gene
+        for gene in required
+        if gene in NOT_READABLE_FROM_VCF
+        or gene not in report.genes
+        or not report.genes[gene].sufficient
+    )
+    return DrugEligibility(
+        drug=drug.strip().lower(),
+        required_genes=required,
+        failed_genes=failed,
+        guidance_capable=bool(capability and capability.guidance_capable),
+        limitation=capability.limitation if capability else "",
+    )
+
+
+def _evidence_reason(cov: GeneCoverage) -> str:
+    labels = {
+        EvidenceStatus.PARTIAL.value: "partial genotypes",
+        EvidenceStatus.NO_CALL.value: "no-calls",
+        EvidenceStatus.GT_NOT_PROVIDED.value: "rows without GT",
+        EvidenceStatus.FILTER_FAILED.value: "FILTER-failed calls",
+        EvidenceStatus.QUALITY_NOT_ESTABLISHED.value: "unfiltered calls",
+        EvidenceStatus.MALFORMED.value: "malformed genotypes",
+        EvidenceStatus.ABSENT.value: "absent positions",
+    }
+    detail = [
+        f"{cov.evidence_counts.get(status, 0)} {label}"
+        for status, label in labels.items()
+        if cov.evidence_counts.get(status, 0)
+    ]
+    suffix = f" Evidence gaps: {', '.join(detail)}." if detail else ""
+    return (
+        f"{cov.present} of {cov.required} required positions had acceptable "
+        f"genotypes; this gene needs {cov.min_percent}%." + suffix
+    )
+
+

+def _upload_too_large(size: int) -> ApiError:
+    return ApiError(
+        413,
+        "FILE_TOO_LARGE",
+        f"The file is over the {MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES // (1024 * 1024)} MB upload "
+        "limit. Restrict it to the required pharmacogenomic positions before "
+        "uploading.",
+    )
+
+
+async def _read_upload_limited(file: UploadFile) -> bytes:
+    """Read at most the declared upload cap plus one byte, then always close."""
+    declared_size = getattr(file, "size", None)
+    if isinstance(declared_size, int) and declared_size > MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES:
+        await file.close()
+        raise _upload_too_large(declared_size)
+
+    contents = bytearray()
+    try:
+        while len(contents) <= MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES:
+            remaining = MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES + 1 - len(contents)
+            chunk = await file.read(min(UPLOAD_READ_CHUNK_BYTES, remaining))
+            if not chunk:
+                break
+            contents.extend(chunk)
+        if len(contents) > MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES:
+            raise _upload_too_large(len(contents))
+        return bytes(contents)
+    finally:
+        await file.close()
+
+
+def _workload_decision(request: Request) -> security.RateLimitDecision:
+    """Shared public workload budget for preview and final analysis."""
+    if not security.should_rate_limit(request):
+        return security.RateLimitDecision(True, security.RATE_LIMIT_REQUESTS, 0)
+    return security.limiter.check(security.client_key(request))
+

+async def _exec(command: list[str]) -> PharmcatInvocation:
+    """
+    Run `command` under the concurrency gate, enforcing the timeout.
+
+    This inner gate owns JVM execution and cleanup. The outer ASGI admission
+    gate serializes /analyze before multipart parsing and VCF decompression,
+    because multiple queued expanded uploads exceeded the 512 MiB cgroup.
+    """
+    async with _pharmcat_slot():
+        return await _exec_unguarded(command)
+
+
+def _signal_owned_process(process: asyncio.subprocess.Process, sig: signal.Signals) -> None:
+    """Signal only the process tree created for this invocation."""
+    try:
+        if os.name == "posix":
+            os.killpg(process.pid, sig)
+        elif sig == signal.SIGTERM:
+            process.terminate()
+        else:
+            process.kill()
+    except ProcessLookupError:
+        pass
+
+
+async def _terminate_owned_process(process: asyncio.subprocess.Process) -> None:
+    """Terminate, escalate if needed, and reap an owned child process."""
+    if process.returncode is not None:
+        await process.wait()
+        return
+    _signal_owned_process(process, signal.SIGTERM)
+    try:
+        await asyncio.wait_for(
+            process.wait(), timeout=PHARMCAT_TERMINATION_GRACE_SECONDS
+        )
+    except asyncio.TimeoutError:
+        pass
+    # The direct child may exit while a descendant ignores SIGTERM. The process
+    # group remains ours, so send the final signal to the group unconditionally.
+    _signal_owned_process(process, signal.SIGKILL)
+    await process.wait()
+
+
+async def _stop_completed_process_descendants(
+    process: asyncio.subprocess.Process,
+) -> None:
+    """Remove any daemonized descendants left after the direct child exits."""
+    if os.name != "posix":
+        return
+    try:
+        os.killpg(process.pid, 0)
+    except ProcessLookupError:
+        return
+    _signal_owned_process(process, signal.SIGTERM)
+    await asyncio.sleep(min(PHARMCAT_TERMINATION_GRACE_SECONDS, 0.1))
+    _signal_owned_process(process, signal.SIGKILL)
+
+

             *command,

             stderr=asyncio.subprocess.PIPE,
+            cwd=cwd,
+            start_new_session=(os.name == "posix"),
         )
     except FileNotFoundError as exc:
         # resolve_invoker() already confirmed the executable existed, so reaching
@@ -476,14 +536,19 @@ async def _exec_unguarded(command: list[str]) -> PharmcatInvocation:

+class ProxyTrustConfigurationError(RuntimeError):
+    """The trusted-proxy allowlist is invalid."""
+
+
+def trusted_proxy_networks() -> tuple[ipaddress.IPv4Network | ipaddress.IPv6Network, ...]:
+    """Networks allowed to supply sanitized forwarding headers."""
+    raw = os.environ.get("TRUSTED_PROXY_CIDRS", "")
+    networks = []
+    for item in raw.split(","):
+        value = item.strip()
+        if not value:
+            continue
+        try:
+            networks.append(ipaddress.ip_network(value, strict=False))
+        except ValueError as exc:
+            raise ProxyTrustConfigurationError(
+                f"Invalid TRUSTED_PROXY_CIDRS entry {value!r}; use an IP or CIDR."
+            ) from exc
+    return tuple(networks)
+
+
+def assert_proxy_trust_configured() -> None:
+    """Validate proxy trust at startup rather than on the first request."""
+    trusted_proxy_networks()
+
+
+def _peer_is_trusted_proxy(host: str) -> bool:
+    try:
+        address = ipaddress.ip_address(host)
+    except ValueError:
+        return False
+    return any(address in network for network in trusted_proxy_networks())
+
+
+def proxy_trust_summary() -> dict[str, object]:
+    networks = trusted_proxy_networks()
+    return {
+        "forwarded_headers": "trusted-peers-only" if networks else "ignored",
+        "trusted_proxy_cidrs": [str(network) for network in networks],
+    }

+class AnalysisMemoryGateMiddleware:
+    """Admit one upload before multipart parsing and VCF decompression.
+
+    The PharmCAT semaphore still owns JVM execution and cleanup. This outer
+    gate covers both /analyze and /coverage: either can expand a real-sized
+    VCF while a JVM consumes the container's remaining memory.
+    """
+
+    def __init__(self, app, wait_seconds: float):
+        self.app = app
+        self.wait_seconds = wait_seconds
+        # A process-wide lock is safe across the separate event loops used by
+        # embedded ASGI clients, while never blocking the server's event loop.
+        self._state_lock = threading.Lock()
+        self._waiters = deque()
+        self._busy = False
+
+    async def __call__(self, scope, receive, send):
+        if (scope["type"] != "http" or scope.get("method") != "POST"
+                or scope.get("path") not in {"/analyze", "/coverage"}):
+            await self.app(scope, receive, send)
+            return
+
+        ticket = object()
+        with self._state_lock:
+            self._waiters.append(ticket)
+        acquired = False
+        deadline = time.monotonic() + self.wait_seconds
+        try:
+            while True:
+                with self._state_lock:
+                    if not self._busy and self._waiters[0] is ticket:
+                        self._waiters.popleft()
+                        self._busy = acquired = True
+                if acquired:
+                    break
+                remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
+                if remaining <= 0:
+                    response = JSONResponse(
+                        status_code=503,
+                        content={
+                            "detail": (
+                                "The server is busy with another analysis and "
+                                "could not start yours in time. Nothing is wrong "
+                                "with your file. Please try again in a moment."
+                            ),
+                            "error_code": "SERVER_BUSY",
+                        },
+                        headers={"Retry-After": str(max(1, int(self.wait_seconds)))},
+                    )
+                    await response(scope, receive, send)
+                    return
+                await asyncio.sleep(min(0.01, remaining))
+            await self.app(scope, receive, send)
+        finally:
+            with self._state_lock:
+                if acquired:
+                    self._busy = False
+                else:
+                    self._waiters.remove(ticket)
+
+

+def _declared_builds(header_lines: list[str]) -> set[str]:
+    """Every build asserted by authoritative header fields.
+
+    Keeping the full set is essential: collapsing contradictory declarations
+    to ``Unknown`` or preferring the convenient build would turn a broken file
+    into an apparently usable one.
+    """
+    relevant = [
+        line
+        for line in header_lines
+        if line.startswith(("##reference", "##contig", "##assembly"))
+    ]
+    table = _build_evidence().get("accession_to_build", {})
+    seen: set[str] = set()
+    for line in relevant:
+        for accession in _ACCESSION.findall(line):
+            build = table.get(accession)
+            if build:
+                seen.add(build)
+
+    blob = "\n".join(relevant)
+    if any(pattern.search(blob) for pattern in _GRCH37_PATTERNS):
+        seen.add("GRCh37")
+    if any(pattern.search(blob) for pattern in _GRCH38_PATTERNS):
+        seen.add("GRCh38")
+    return seen
+

 ## Where usable files come from — and where they do not
 
@@ -97,6 +99,30 @@ a clinical PGx panel or unfiltered whole-genome/exome calling produces.
 indistinguishable, to the matcher, from a file where those positions were never

+### Evidence-quality contract
+
+For this application, a required position is usable only when the sample's `GT`
+value is located from the row's `FORMAT` field and is a complete genotype (for
+example `0/0`, `0/1`, or a phased equivalent). A named FILTER failure such as
+`LowQual` is not usable evidence.
+
+The following remain distinct in the reported evidence census and do **not**
+satisfy completeness: an absent position; a no-call such as `./.`; a partial call
+such as `0/.`; a row whose `FORMAT` has no `GT`; a malformed genotype; a named
+FILTER failure such as `LowQual`. The system does not infer an omitted position
+as reference.
+
+No universal DP, GQ, or QUAL cutoff is imposed. Those values depend on assay and
+caller. The application trusts `FILTER=PASS`, rejects named FILTER failures, and
+classifies `FILTER=.` separately as quality not established. A complete genotype
+on such a row remains usable because the VCF has not explicitly failed it, but
+both preview and final analysis emit a warning telling the reader to confirm the
+upstream caller's quality policy.
+
+Uploads must contain exactly one sample. Multi-sample VCFs are rejected with a
+specific error so the caller can split them deliberately rather than having the
+application silently choose the first sample.
+
 `pharmcat_positions_3.4.0.vcf` is fetched by
 `scripts/fetch_reference_data.py --fetch-tools` and lists every required position.

@@ -590,12 +599,16 @@ API from a visitor's browser. Phase 4 names origins explicitly, and
 
 ### Rate limiter honesty
 
-It is in-memory and keyed on `X-Forwarded-For`, which is caller-controlled. That
-is abuse *dampening*, not a security boundary — it stops a casual scraper
-burning the free-tier budget. It resets on container restart, which on a
-scale-to-zero host is often. Real protection needs a shared store and a trusted
-proxy; both are out of scope here, and pretending otherwise would be worse than
-saying so.
+It is in-memory and resets on container restart, which on a scale-to-zero host
+is often. Forwarded identity is ignored unless the direct peer matches
+`TRUSTED_PROXY_CIDRS`; only list proxy networks that guarantee sanitization of
+incoming forwarding headers. With the safe empty default, callers behind one
+proxy share a budget. That is abuse *dampening*, not a security boundary. A
+multi-instance deployment would need a shared store, which remains out of scope.
+
+The ASGI server's forwarded-header trust must match the application allowlist.
+Do not configure either side to trust every address merely to recover per-user
+limits; that restores caller-controlled identity.

 ## Data privacy
 
-**No genomic data is retained.** An uploaded VCF is held in memory and written
-to a per-request temporary directory that is deleted in a `finally` block before
-the response is returned. Nothing is logged, persisted, or sent anywhere else.

-and [asserted by a test](https://github.com/bhuvantharanath/pharmaguard/blob/main/backend/tests/test_deployment.py).

+in memory and written to an owned per-request temporary directory. The PharmCAT
+process group is stopped/reaped and its VCF, report, and tool-log tree is deleted
+before the concurrency slot is returned. Static/default explanation mode makes
+no outbound explanation call. This does not claim control over Spaces access
+logs, crash dumps, backups, or other platform-level retention.
